# Todo — bayan

The unscheduled backlog: everything known to be worth doing that nobody has committed to a date.
Items enter here from anywhere (a review comment, an out-of-scope note, a bug found in passing)
and leave by being promoted into `status/next.md` or dropped with a reason.

Keep it honest rather than tidy. A backlog that only contains things someone intends to do is a
wish list; the value is in recording the ones nobody has got to yet.

## Now-ish

Small, clear, and could be picked up without a design decision.

- ~~CI: install zig (or set `BAYAN_CC`) on all three runners so `run/` goldens execute there.~~ (done: ci.yml:40-42 installs zig; triage 2026-11-09)
- `bayan build --emit-c file.byn` (0.3.1) leaves a stray `a.o` in the current directory (README v3 writer, 2026-11-16).
- Spec §21.4 says `bayan dist` writes `dist/<version>/<target>/`; 0.3.1 writes `dist/<version>/` and `dist/latest/` (README follows the binary). Reconcile spec or code.
- `bayan --help` lists `parse [--ast] <file>`, but plain `bayan parse` is refused by the self-hosted compiler while `parse --ast` works; settle in the help rewrite (review of 9e54c2e..cf32c38).
- `docs/export/BAYAN-DIAGNOSTICS.md` and `BAYAN-GRAMMAR.md` still say BYN5014 is "not a known verb or noun"; regenerate the export snapshot.
- `#line`: function signatures and prologues map to `main.c`, so a debugger breakpoint on a function names generated code until its first statement; mapping the signature to the declaration line would fix it.
- `CliToolchainTests.GeneratedCTheCCompilerRejectsIsAnInternalCompilerError` uses a `/bin/sh` fake compiler and returns early on Windows.
- Float-to-int conversion saturates in `bayan-rt.h` (`byn_f2i`) instead of trapping as SPEC §6.2 says.
- `i64.tryFrom` of exactly -2^63 as a float gives `null`; the float range check is `(min-1, max+1)` in `f64`, which rounds at that edge.
- `let e: u8[0] = []` is BYN4001: the C compiler rejects the emitted "initializer for aggregate with no elements".
- A generic instantiated once and called from several places reports its instance BYN7012 (Q342) only at the first call; the instance is cached per type arguments.
- `docs/bayan-milestones.md:218` says `--ci github` will run `bayan fmt --check` and `bayan fmt --check-prose`; the command is `bayan format`, and the CI template does not run it yet.
- `if a == b` emits `if ((x == y))`, which clang rejects under `-Wall -Werror` (`-Wparentheses-equality`); L016's generator uses `<` to avoid it (008 T10).
- A tuple-typed struct field (`(i8, i64) f1`) is BYN1001: the parser reads it as a function type; L016's generator nests only structs in structs (008 T10).
- `src/lexer/diagnostics.byn:205` `wrap` splits at every space, including inside backticks, while C# `DiagnosticRenderer.Wrap` keeps backticked code one word, so a diagnostic whose prose holds multi-word code can wrap differently; L017's random corpus counts 1 such wrapping-only dump per seed and compares the other 2047 (Q505).
- 009 T18 (L018 proof): C#'s own spans break child-within-parent on 30 of 128 corrupted and 1,085 of 4,000 random inputs (measured 2026-11-03; the `move`/`fail`/labeled `break`/`continue`/`except`/`?.` spans and the dropped-value `ErrorExpr`); the 30 corrupted inputs and the 1,085 random indices are listed in `docs/laws/018-no-parser-panic.md`. No input crashes the C# parser (the two `ReportBraceless` crashes, `0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 02184` and `02482`, were fixed in 1127c91 and the port in 010 T44). L018 stays proposed on the child-within-parent count (Q509); enactment is scheduled for the start of 011 (Q744).
- The port reads a non-ASCII uppercase name before `{` as a plain name: `\u00C5{}` (`Å{}`) dumps `Expr Å` in `bayan-parse` and `Expr (struct Å)` in C# (`É{}` too; `A{}` and `å{}` agree; first seen as L017 random-a 01877, `\u212B    {`, Q889). fixing task: 010 T28
- The C# checker throws on a property named like another member: `struct S{\ni8 x\ni8 x=>1}` gives `InvalidOperationException: Sequence contains no matching element` at `Compilation.BindBody` (`csharp/Bayan.Semantics/Compilation.cs:1820`, `Properties.First(p => p.Getter == fn)`); two same-named properties throw too (first seen as L023 random/08565, Q889). fixing task: own fix/ branch off dev (C# checker crash)
- ~~Schemata.Rebinds (csharp/Bayan.Testing/Schemata.cs:69,132) re-binds the whole program per site via Compilation.WithFile; plan 013-plan.md:27 says per enclosing statement. Scaling cost (review 9629643fd1f5..c825a0796a20).~~ (superseded by the Schemata.Rebinds entry below; triage 2026-11-09)
- Coverage drop check: land.sh only runs quality-baseline.py update because a filtered per-task run can only raise recorded coverage; the drop check waits for a full-suite measurement.
- Hermetic zig (user 2026-11-08): the build fetches the pinned zig into a repo or cache directory (e.g. `tools/zig/0.17.0/`, gitignored, via `scripts/ci/install-zig.sh`) and `Toolchain` prefers it over PATH, so a Homebrew upgrade (0.17.1 on mb1, 2026-11-08) cannot break `ZigVersionPinTests` or the cross builds.
- C# lowerer (open, found in 017 T10, recorded as the second paragraph of Q1100): `only() catch (G.a) 4` in a non-failing function, where `only` fails `G` whose only variant is `a`, lowers the propagation of the uncaught variants as `return t1;` (`byn_err`) in an `int64_t` function and clang rejects it; repro `tmp/probe/catchv/main.byn`. Conservative fix: a variant catch that covers the whole set drops the propagation (C# `Lowerer`, then the selfhost mirror). Owner: none yet.

- `bayan run` on a single file compiles every `.byn` in that file's directory (found in M15, spec 001).

- `using` on a `Map` is BYN2226 while `List` allows it (M20, spec 001).

- Generics (M19): `mod.f<T>(…)` type args on a member expression not wired; shape checks (borrowed fields, recursion) skip instances; polymorphic recursion (`f<T>` → `f<List<T>>`) unbounded; no target-type inference (`Option2.Nothing`, `Pair` without args); generic struct formats as `Pair { … }` without type args; `print(x)` with `x: T` rejected (use `$"{x}"`)
- Misplaced doc comment above `BoundVariantGroup` in `BinderExpressions.cs` (belongs to `BoundBuiltinGroup`)
- Still flaky under load after the test-time-scale work (2026-11-08): `RunGolden(run/assert-fails.test.byn)` and the CLI-driven `ExternalModeTests`/`MutationRunnerTests` cases run `bayan test` in-process, so the per-test timeout is the src default, which `BAYAN_TEST_TIME_SCALE` does not reach; `ParserTests.TheLineJoinLintIsLinear` and `RegionAnalysisPerfTests` are wall-clock perf bounds and fail on a loaded machine at scale 1.
- `List<T>` copied by value (`var b = a; b.add(…)` ×N) shares and reallocs storage: heap use-after-free under the default allocator (ASan, review of spec 001); pre-existing since v0.1, needs `unique`/`move` (spec §9) or a BYN2762-style copy ban
- `Io.readText` abandons the whole file buffer from `context.allocator` when the bytes are not UTF-8 (`bayan-rt.c` read path)
- `Io.run` (POSIX): only the pipe fds are `FD_CLOEXEC`; `byn_io_open` uses `fopen` without `"e"`, so open files are inherited by children
- Flow analysis: `x ??= return 3;` followed by code gives a false BYN2405 "unreachable code" warning (`x = x ?? return 3;` does not); found in the spec 002 loop-3 review
- L005 proof never runs a real `cmd.exe`: the read-back model in `csharp/tests/Bayan.Tests/L005RunBatchTests.cs` encodes cmd rules by hand, so a quoted character cmd.exe or code page 65001 reads differently would pass; needs a Windows run of the generated cases through `cmd.exe` and a `%~1`/`shift` batch file (review of feat/laws-005-007)
- `map[k].clear()` and `map[k].add(x)` report BYN2106: a `map[k]` receiver is a write target only for `take()` (spec 003 T1); other mutating builtins on a map element need the same `MapWriteTarget` treatment
- Unverified (spec 003 review, finding F): `StrBuf.take()` refills the place using the current `byn_context` allocator, and RegionAnalysis does not see the `StrBufGrow` the lowering introduces; check whether a `take()` on a `ref StrBuf` from an outer region allocates the refill in the wrong region
- `_ = move a;` reports BYN2001, while BYN1012's help (for a bare `move a;`) suggests `_ = f();`; either accept a discarded move or change the help text
- Spec 003 review loop 3: `CEmitter` skips the after-call claim of a root moved anywhere in the statement, even on a branch that does not run (`let n = flag ? consume(move b) : b.grow();`): storage grown on the other branch stays unclaimed; no failing program found
- xunit `Assert.Contains`/`DoesNotContain` on strings is culture-sensitive, so ICU ignores `\u001b` and `"\u001b["` matches any `[`: the older escape checks in `CliEnvironmentTests` (e.g. `ColorFollowsTheTerminalNoColorAndTheFlag`) can pass or fail for the wrong reason; pass `StringComparison.Ordinal` (found in spec 005 T3)
- Spec 004 R4: a discarded first pass now drops the generic function instances it created, but not generic struct/union instances (`InstantiateType`), which can stay as unused `i32` instantiations
- Spec 004 R4: a value derived from a pending `var` through a call (`n = id(n);`, generic `T` inferred from `n`) counts as a typed `i32` use and fixes `n` at `i32`
- Spec 004 R4: an optional integer target (`i64?`) does not count as a constraining use, so a later `u8` use can still retype the local
- `SelfhostLexerTests` (`StdinRuns`, sequential `bayan-lex` processes) did not finish within 10 minutes while several worktrees ran `dotnet test` at once (spec 004 T9); the rest of the suite took about 75 s, and running `bayan-lex --json` by hand on `tests/cases/lex/*.byn` exits right away
- An untyped array literal used directly as a `for` source (`for (let w in ["a", "b"])`) gives BYN2218 "needs a known type here"; infer the element type (found in 004 T15).
- Spec §21.2 CLI table lists neither `bayan check`, `lex` and `parse` nor the new `types` (spec 004 T16)
- R4 literal inference (spec 004 review): retyping a `var` at its first constraining use can break a later use that only compiled by widening (`var n = 0; i64 big = n; takeI32(n);` → BYN2202) and silently changes overflow behaviour (`var n = 2147483647; i64 big = n; n += 1;` no longer panics). Consider a lint or requiring all constraining uses to agree.
- R6 receiver borrows (spec 004 review): reassigning the whole receiver (`b = Buf{…}`) while a span from `b.view()` is alive is not BYN3004 (memory-safe, same as the direct `b.data.span` form).
- `List.removeRange` on a list of unique elements does not free the removed elements (same as `clear`; spec 004 review).
- R4 pending-var groups resolve by statement order (`var a = 0; var b = 0; i64 x = a; u8 y = b; a = b;` is BYN2202, swapped declarations compile); a widest-fitting choice would remove the order dependence (review loop 2, 004).
- ~~`LexerTests.OneMegabyteAllocatesOnlyTheTokenArray` fails when other test suites run concurrently on the machine (per-thread allocation count); passes alone. Seen 4 times during the 004/005 runs.~~ (superseded by the later OneMegabyteAllocatesOnlyTheTokenArray entry; triage 2026-11-09)
- R4 pending-var groups with mixed uses: `var a = 0; var b = 0; a = b; f64 y = a; u8 x = b;` is BYN2202 (group falls back to i32). Needs a rule that picks a type converting to every member's use (u8 here); neither "first integer constraint" (breaks i64+f64) nor "fall back to i32" (breaks u8) works. Dev rejects it too (review loop 3, 004).
- Round 2 §1: spec §3.12-3.14 still show PascalCase union cases and no case values; `flags` is still lexed (spec-only part of the plan, not done in the unions compiler task).
- Map literals do not diagnose duplicate constant keys (`["a": 1, "a": 2]` keeps the last); spread element types must match the literal's exactly (no `i32` → `i64` widening per element) (round 2 §11 literals).
- HANDOFF §10/§18: SPEC §19.2 manifest example and `docs/diagnostics/BYN310x.md` examples still use `field = value`; `examples/*/` have no `package.byn` to migrate (r2-colon step was code only).

- Panic handlers (BYN2612) are not checked for allocation inside user functions they call; only direct allocations in the handler body or the `Panic.handler` function are reported.
- BYN2613 accepts a `let` moved after the handler registers (`let xs = …; defer panicking print($"{xs.length}"); take(move xs);`); the handler would read a moved value.

- `Messages` (005 T4) formats with the current culture to stay byte-identical; `DiagnosticDescriptor.Format` uses the invariant culture, so `{1:0.0} MB` differs by locale (decide when translations land).
- R15 (005 T15): `HelpLanguageTests` scans help literals right after `help:`/`help =`; ternary branches and help variables built from lists (e.g. `OwnershipAnalysis` options) are only covered through the goldens, and CLI `stderr.WriteLine("  help: …")` lines in `Dist.cs`/`Scaffold.cs` are not scanned.
- `bayan test` (005 T12): the "top three stack frames" trim is untested end to end because the runtime prints one `at file:line` frame per panic.
- The error cap line (`… and N more errors`) follows the last diagnostic with no blank line between them (`tests/cases/style/diag-cap.*`); the guide does not say which (005 T14).
- `bayan test -v` pass lines have no per-test timing (spec 005 R8 asked for one)
- `bayan test -v` pending line is `  ◆ <module>: <name> (pending)`, not the planned `  pending <module>: <name>: <message>` (spec 005)
- Diagnostics for loose files show absolute paths in `-->` (spec 005 R4: relative to the package root)

- `ref this` on a constructor falls through to the `ref` modifier with type `this` instead of a targeted error (round 2 §9).
- Postfix `break if (x);` parentheses are not reported as an input form; BYN1030 omits `sbyte`, `ushort`, `nint`, `nuint`; `RecoverIfNeeded` can resync into follow-on errors after an input-form error; the dangling-`else` case of BYN1017 is unchecked (round 2 §15).
- The binder keeps `c.Binding` / `IsCatch` paths for `catch (e)` that only erroneous input reaches now (round 2 §15).
- No golden covers the BYN2218 `NoTargetType` help (round 2 §15).
- `tests/cases/run/condition-cast.byn` line 10 prints "parenthesized if" but is now `if flag { … }`; only the cast lines cover paren-vs-cast (round 2 §15).
- Scaffold templates and `tests/cases/new/*.tree` still use `assert(...)`; move to `assert x;` (round 2 §16).
- Power-assert: string values print unquoted; column math ignores tabs and wide characters; guarded pieces emit one `if` each, so C size grows with the square of the lazy-column count (round 2 §16).
- Spec §20 examples and "assertion failed" wording in older docs and plans are not migrated to power-assert (round 2 §16).
- `tryFirst` has a BYN2230 row in the grammar table but no `ReportRenamed` mapping; the BYN2230 help for `asString()` says "rename it `finish`" without `()` (round 2 §11).
- `InterpolationSource` (`BinderExpressions.cs:865`) may cut `"""` raw strings wrong, giving a broken BYN2209 fix; BYN2201 help rows were misaligned (r2 stdlib gate; recheck).
- r2-finalfix: `unless (!x) print("f"); else print("g");` leaves neither BYN1007 nor BYN1018 with a fix (BYN1006 already forces a rewrite).
- r2-finalfix: `if ((!x)) { … }` gets no BYN1032; `ParseIf` (`Parser.cs:1302`) tests the condition without `Unwrap`.
- `Parser.cs` `ParseDefer` (~:1585) still passes `clean = ErrorCount == errors` to ReportBraceless while ParseBody uses "no inner diagnostic has a fix": `defer let x = <expr with a fixless error>;` loses its braces fix (review of fix/review-2026-10-22-1)
- `unless (a == null) s;` on a `bool` `a` reports BYN1017/1018/1006 but no BYN1023 (review of fix/review-2026-10-22-1, probe p12)
- A field witnessing a getter declared with `fails` may get a vtable thunk that ignores the `fails` return convention (`CEmitter.cs` Vtable). Unprobed; flagged by the 006-fixa writer.
- Region analysis runs on generic instances only, so a never-instantiated generic struct's methods get no BYN3004 (audit 2026-10-23 probe p7); instantiated ones match the non-generic case.
- BYN2202 on a generic method's type parameter reads "expected `U`, found `U`" (two distinct `U`s print alike).
- Reassigning an owner (`xs = List<i64>()`, `this.items = …` inside `for x in items`) while a span or loop borrows it records no mutation; memory-safe only because lists live in arenas and reassignment frees nothing (probes p4b/p8/p9 of the 006 final review, 2026-10-23). Becomes a hole if reassignment ever frees.
- `RegionAnalysis.DirectChanges` (used by `ChangedFields`) does not count a `dyn ref` local that borrows a `this` field and is passed on to a writable `dyn ref` parameter (006-fixj note).
- A `dyn ref` assigned from anything but a ToDynRef conversion, a tracked local or a `for` variable is assumed to hold borrowable storage, so passing it to a writable `dyn ref` parameter can give a false BYN3004 (006-fixj note).
- `docs/decisions/2026-11-14-004-answered-questions.md`: Question numbers repeat (two 51–56 runs, the "(entry 2)" rows); renumber or switch to ids.
- `bayan format <dir>` recurses into everything (`tmp/`, `out/`, hidden dirs, and `tests/cases` fixtures that use input forms on purpose); decide on exclusions (review of bcc6c2f).
- SPEC §18 writes pointers after the type (`Sqlite3* db`); whether they go first is open (docs/decisions/2026-11-14-004-answered-questions.md Question 120).
- r3-51a: the BYN1053 message says "one line" even when the comma-separated cases span several lines.
- r3-51a: a line starting with `?`, `:` or `|` is not waṣl, so a ternary or pattern split before its operator ends the statement.
- r3-51a: a labeled statement as the first line of a `catch { ... }` brace body is ambiguous with a struct-literal field.
- r3-51a: the C-style `for (init; cond; step)` header keeps its `;`; decide whether it is an input form too.
- r3-51a: `if cond` with `{` on the next line reads the brace as a stranded postfix instead of reporting the brace placement.
- r3-51a: two declarations on one line after `}` (`int f() { return 0 } void g() {`) give no BYN1005.
- r3-51a: `return` then a call at the same indent on the next line is a bare return plus an unreachable statement (BYN2405), not BYN1042; by design, check the docs agree.
- r3-51a: tests/cases/diag and parse `input-forms-one-line` no longer fit on one line after expand-blocks; rename or reshape.
- Waṣl clause lines `uses`, `expects`, `except`, `becomes` (J61): no parser support yet; add a continuation test with each clause's parser step (5.1b–5.3). From r3-51a gate.
- r3-suq-paper: 16 `suq/fail/` snippets have no allocated code (listed "none allocated" in `suq/fail/README.md`: attr-case, byte-count, dangling-blank, drop-ref-this, extern-string, in-convert, keyword-name, leading-operator, log-at, mode-particle, on-field, owned-copied, try-catch, try-literal, unit-round, using-let); allocate or map them.
- BYN2226 (`using` not disposable) and its doc stay until the step that drops `using` per C46; then retire the code and rewrite `docs/diagnostics/BYN2226.md`.
- ~~r3-51b1: the BYN1043 local fix picks `let`/`var` by a syntactic scan, so a local changed only through a mutating method gets `let` and then BYN2104; move the choice to the binder (Q122).~~ (superseded (its own follow-up says nothing to build); triage 2026-11-09)
- r3-51b1: C# test strings still holding type-first locals are found only when a test fails; a sweep of `csharp/tests/Bayan.Tests/*.cs` program strings for `T x = e` would catch any that expect errors.
- r3-51b1: `IsWrittenLater` scans to the enclosing `}`, so a top-level script local is scanned to end of file and a same-named local in a later function counts as a write.
- r3-51b2a: messages and docs still say `error enum`/`unique struct`: BYN2608, BYN2609, the BYN2804 "an error enum" kind, the `[ExitCode]` help in `Compilation.cs`, and `TypeFacts.WhyUnique` ("declared `unique struct`"); the pages under `docs/diagnostics/` that show `error enum` examples.
- r3-51b2a: `ErrorEnumDecl`/`ErrorSetDecl` were kept (AST dump `ErrorEnum`/`ErrorSet`) instead of the plan's single `ErrorUnionDecl`; merge them if the binder needs one node.
- r3-51b2a: `except` narrowing of a `union error` is rejected with BYN2022 until function error sets carry excluded cases (Q124).
- r3-51b2a gate: BYN3202 in a nested composition is reported once per composed alias that flattens it (CheckComposition runs per alias); report once at the innermost head.
- r3-51b2a gate: `union error Name<T>` type parameters do not parse; needed for HANDOFF §11 `ValidationError<T>`.
- r3-51b2b: test parameters already parsed type-first with `[Cases]` binding (round 2); 5.1b added no code for them beyond the BYN1043 input form from part 1.
- r3-51b2b: J46 "no `uses`" rule for computed properties (expression or block body) waits for `uses` (5.1d).
- r3-51b2b: `const x: T = pattern …` (a typed pattern const) parses; no pattern type exists to check `T` against.
- r3-51b2b gate: `suq/orders/order.byn:32` and `suq/storefront/catalog.byn:29` write `public const x = pattern …`, which is BYN1001 (`public` then `const`); fix the paper app or the grammar.
- r3-51b2b gate: `ref this is .s` after other parameters (already BYN2117) leaves `is .s` unconsumed and adds a second parse error.
- r3-51c: BYN0007/0008/0009 messages, `TokenKind.CharLiteral` and `PrimitiveKind.Char` still say char; rename in the C# and selfhost lexers together (Q133).
- r3-51c: `unsafe because` (J6) waits for an `unsafe` block statement; none parses today.
- r3-51c: BYN2322's help still suggests a function's `requires`, which becomes `expects` (BYN1063, 5.1e).
- r3-51c: C17 lists `rune` as a keyword; 5.1c keeps it a type name like `bool`.
- r3-51c: `suq/fail/{bracket-attr,block-comment,char,requires-value}.byn` keep their input forms on purpose (compile-fail corpus).
- `tests/cases/diag/input-negated-if.byn` and `input-paren-negation.byn` keep `input-` names though BYN1032 is retired (5.1c); rename or move to a non-diag case.
- r3-52b: SPEC §21.2 `bayan format` row still says "not in v0.1"; 5.2b ships `--check` and `style` (C16).
- r3-q140: types printed in diagnostics are not yet rendered in the project style (`style.primitives`); SPEC §2 line 118 still says the C# primitive names are lexer-reserved in every project, which the lexer does not do.
- r3-52b: `tests/cases/typed/rune.byn` and `parse/word-booleans.byn` use word booleans, which `bayan format` (default `.symbols`) rewrites; fine for goldens, but not in project style.
- Revision 0001: postfix pointers (J91, BYN1066) and `#line` mapping (J93) have no plan §4 row; scheduled as steps after 5.1e (pointers, `Parser.cs`) and in parallel now (`#line`, `CEmitter.cs`). C# primitive names are not lexer-reserved yet (J92).
- r3-51d: the `effect` keyword is still reserved.
- r3-51d: `ref from Buf` (particle after the passing mode, `suq/fail/mode-particle.byn`) misparses with no diagnostic code.
- r3-51d: the parser has no function types, so `uses` inside them is not handled.
- `suq/fail/README.md:20` lists `csharp-alias.byn` under BYN1030, retired by revision 0001 (J92); move it out of the compile-fail corpus.
- Revision 0002 item 16: the project-style type printer (with `TypeResolver.cs`) was not built in 5.2b; follow-up, after 5.2b.
- Revision 0002 item 25: BYN1042 (built in 5.1a) does not cover `fail`; add `fail` to its scope and message in 5.1e.
- Revision 0002 item 1: BYN1065 (clause order) does not know `expects`; order `where` → `expects` → `becomes` → `fails` → `uses` and the new message in 5.1e.
- Revision 0002 item 27: 5.1c moves a field rule that reads `it` and another field to the struct (Q131); 5.3b rejects it under BYN3218 instead (revision 0004 item 1).
- Revision 0002 item 8: J55 now adopts the BYN1043 syntactic scan (Q122), so the r3-51b1 entry above (move the choice to the binder) is superseded; nothing to build.
- Revision 0002 item 9: `except .case` stays parse only with BYN2022 (Q124); 5.3b lifts it.
- Revision 0002 item 16: the C# primitive names are reserved in `Keywords.cs` in 5.1e (the revision 0001 entry above, J92).
- Revision 0003: the built `string.from`/`string.tryFrom`/`string.invalidUtf8At` (Q71, `Builtins.cs:47-51`) predate the codecs; `invalidUtf8At` is dropped (revision 0004 item 5); rename to `Utf8.decode`/`Utf8.tryDecode` under BYN2230 in 5.4. Named format specs and BYN1068 parse in 5.1e.
- Revision 0005: the BYN5001 checker (`csharp/Bayan.Semantics`) rejects an `-ed` member with no `ref this` verb; change it in 5.4 to reject an `-ed` member that takes `ref this` and accept one with no plain verb.
- r3-52a: `struct Point { f64 x, f64 y }` (fields on one line with `,`) is BYN1005 with a fix that leaves `, f64 y }` on the next line; no BYN1053 rewrite for fields.
- r3-52a: BYN2787's page and `trust-reason.byn` still describe `trust(x, reason:)`; decide in 5.3e whether BYN2787 retires once `trust` is only an input form.
- r3-52a: a user method named `trustUnchecked` with no arguments binds as the `Untrusted` unwrap (Question 143).
- plan-strings-check: diagnostics pages for codes landed before 2026-10-24 carry no verbatim message and help lines; backfill at round-3 end so the check passes for every round-3 code.
- r3-55: SPEC §21.2 still says `bayan format` is "not in v0.1", and a paragraph splits its CLI table (the `bayan pkg` … `bayan new test X` rows sit after the `--color` text); fix in a draft revision (SPEC is frozen).
- r3-55: `docs/bayan-terminal-style.md` §4 has no `bayan audit` section, though `StyleTests` renders an `audit` shape (`tests/cases/style/audit.*`).
- r3-55: `bayan audit` names a call bypass by its member only (`trustUnchecked()`), since there is no trim-safe syntax walker in `Bayan.Syntax`; a walker would let it show the full callee (Question 240).
- r3-55: `bayan audit` lists no C sources per dependency (SPEC §19.3); add them when dependencies land.
- plan-strings-check skips BYN3216: its plan entry has a quoted `{2}` gloss between the message and `(label`, which the form regex rejects; reword the entry (revision erratum) or widen the regex (found 2026-10-24, r3-53b).
- r3-53h: `RegionAnalysis.cs:236` and `OwnershipAnalysis` treat `BoundErrorVariant` as a leaf, so moves and borrows in an error payload are not checked.
- r3-53h: `return .case(…)` is not BYN1056; the `return .x` check (5.3f) sees only a bare `.x`.
- r3-53h: error-case payload fields are not dropped or moved: a `List` or other owned payload leaks, and a unique one is not tracked (Question 311).
- r3-54a: the parser's particle list is private (`Parser.cs`), so `Lexicon.Particles` repeats it; expose one list.
- r3-54a: the BYN5001, BYN5101 and BYN5102 page examples still end field lines with `;`.
- r3-54a: the manifest's invalid-value help does not mention the `[key: value]` map form that `grammar.forms` now uses.
- r3-54a: the task names `node scripts/plan-strings-check.js`, but the script uses Deno APIs; run it with `deno run -A`.
- r3-54a: the BYN51xx exemption for rename attributes (`@json`, `@column`) and `@allow(…) because` is not built (Question 270).
- r3-54a: plan-strings-check skips BYN5001: its message is in revision 0005, not in a plan §2 entry.
- r3-53g: `Conformance.cs` and non-call member lookups take the first method of a name, so an overloaded method cannot satisfy an interface member by its particles (Question 306).
- r3-53g: a generic `rest ReadOnlySpan<T>` does not infer `T` from the tail arguments (`Instantiate` in `BinderCalls.cs` matches parameters one to one).
- r3-53g: `..xs` mixed with other tail arguments (`f(1, ..xs)`) is BYN2215; spreading into a `rest` tail beside values needs the run-time-length span path `FinishCollection` rejects (BYN2219).
- r3-53g: `rest` on a non-span type has no diagnostic; the parser's input forms always build `ReadOnlySpan<T>`, but `rest List<T> xs` is accepted and matched as a plain parameter.
- r3-53g: overloads of the same name each report BYN5001 and other naming lints once per declaration.
- r3-53c1 gate: the BYN7003 second-form and BYN7007 labels underline the state name without its leading dot; plan says "label at `.{0}`".
- review R4/R5: `bayan audit` reuses the `format.io-error` message for its unreadable file and directory lines; a shared `io-error` id would read better.
- review loop 3 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): `Restyle`'s `otherUses` counts struct field declarations and field-init labels, so a file with a field named `i8` never has `i8(x)`/`i8.max` respelled (missed rename only).
- review loop 3 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): a file reached through a symlinked directory keeps its alias path (the `ManifestFor` alias part is fixed in the self-hosted driver, which reads the manifest from the real path; the C# still walks up from the alias); audit output follows stack order, unsorted.
- review loop 3 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): `WriteReplacing` copies only the Unix mode: owner, group, ACLs, xattrs are lost and hard links are split by the rename.
- review loop 4 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): `Format.cs:~363` with `AstDump.cs:~419`: a type-position name is always respelled, so a generic parameter spelled like a primitive (`i8 first<i8>(List<i8> xs)`) becomes `sbyte first<i8>(List<sbyte> xs)` under `primitives: .csharp`.
- review loop 4 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): `RealPath` keeps a link target's letter case, so on case-insensitive APFS `alias -> SRC` beside `src/` gives two dedupe keys: audit lists a bypass twice, format visits the file twice.
- review loop 4 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): The parent-link tests (`FormatTests.cs:~366-373`, `CliTests.cs:~312-316`) pin that `format <dir>` follows `dir/link -> ..` and writes files above `<dir>`; decide whether directory links leaving the named directory should be followed.
- review loop 4 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): `Format.cs` `PackageNames` reads each file by its unresolved path while `within` filters by the resolved one, so a `<link>/..` path can feed names from a different file into the rename guard (missed or extra rename only).
- review loop 4 (2e0c8a49d72e..1b134e57f571): `Format.cs` `ManifestFor` uses `Path.GetFullPath`, so `link/../a.byn` takes its package `style` from the lexical folder, not the folder of the file it reads and writes. Fixed in the self-hosted driver (`walk.byn` `manifestFor` reads the real path); the C# keeps it.
- r3-53c2: an optional of a struct with states (`Order?`) keeps BYN2410 in a `switch`; `.s` arms on it are not checked per state.
- r3-53c2: loops forget what every `var` and `ref` parameter was known to be at loop entry, also when the body never changes it.
- r3-53c2: an `if` whose then-branch makes a `becomes` call forgets the receiver's state after the `if`, even when the else-branch would agree.
- r3-53c2: reading a payload field from several states that hold it after different fields is BYN2022; it needs a per-state read.
- r3-53c2: BYN7006's message (`{0} is .{1}` … write `{2}`) differs from the plan's text (`{0} {1} is .{2}` … write `{0}.{2} {1}`).
- r3-53c2: BYN7007 in a `.s` pattern underlines the name without its dot, like the first half's labels (line above).
- r3-53c2: payload fields are not seen by `TypeFacts`, ownership or drops: a `List` in a state's payload does not make the struct unique and leaks.
- r3-53c2: a struct with states whose own field is named `tag` or `u` collides with the C layout's `tag` and `u` members.
- r3-53c2: a generic struct with states relies on `Compilation.States` holding each instantiation; untested.
- r3-53c2: BYN7009: a `become` inside a loop body does not count after the loop.
- r3-53c2: BYN7003's third form on a `move this` or plain `this` method is followed by BYN2115 at the same call (one extra error).
- `scripts/plan-strings-check.js` parses only `BYN####` entries, so CLI `Messages` strings copied from the plan (e.g. `FormatterHint`, plan §29 (a)) are never checked against it.
- r3-54a-lib: comments in `runtime/bayan-rt.h` and `bayan-rt.c` still say `Panic.onPanic`; rename to `Panic.handler`.
- r3-54a-lib: identifiers still named for round 2: `Compilation.OnPanic`, `BindOnPanic`, `_onPanicSet`, `BoundSetOnPanic`, `DiagnosticCodes.OnPanicPlacement`; rename for `Panic.handler`.
- r3-54a-lib: `tests/cases/run/on-panic-order` prints the label "onPanic:" from its handler; relabel (and rename the case) for `Panic.handler`.
- r3-54a-lib: `suq/fail/to-as-call.byn` keeps `asSpan` by design (a fail-corpus snippet for the `to…`/`as…` member rule); do not convert it.
- r3-54a-lib: `for i in r` over a range held in a `let` crashes the Lowerer (`For` casts the iterable to `ArrayType`); `For` handles only a range literal.
- r3-54a-lib: `mutableSpan()` is exclusive only as a loan (Question 322); a second writable view of the same list while the first lives is not rejected.
- r3-54a-lib: `position`/`lastPosition`/`count`/`replaced` take only a string needle; a rune needle is BYN2202 (Question 319).
- r3-54a-lib: the text views have no `length` (Question 318).
- r3-54a-lib: `T.parse(text)` (fails `ParseError`) is 5.4b; only `T.tryParse(text, as:)` on integers is built (Question 324).
- `string.matches(re)` (SPEC §21 assertions) is not built: no `Regex` type exists (Q326).
- r3-54a-lib3b: list `remove(at:)`, `remove(at: range)` and `remove(from:through:)` do not free removed elements that own memory (Question 329).
- ~~`run/assert-fails.test` and other `run/` goldens fail intermittently under load (parallel workers); they pass on rerun. Find the timing dependency or mark them serial.~~ (superseded by the run/assert-fails.test flake entry above; triage 2026-11-09)
- r3-54b: `suq/storefront/checkout.byn:202` calls `Hex.tryDecode`, which the spec does not name; only `try Hex.decode` exists.
- r3-54b: `Utf8.decode(bytes, invalid: .replace)` allocates 3 bytes per input byte, then shrinks; a counting pass would avoid the over-allocation.
- r3-54b: `for x in b` over a `Range` value that is not a range literal (a bound `.range(let b)`) crashes `Lowerer.cs:689` (InvalidCastException, RangeType to ArrayType); `Range<T>` also has no readers and no format, so `Rule` bounds cannot be observed.
- rv0011: a bare `ValidationError` in a `fails` clause whose body fails reports BYN2023 and then a cascading BYN2603 (the clause resolves to no set); an unknown type there cascades the same way. Suppress BYN2603 when a `fails` member resolved to the error type.
- rv0011: under `BAYAN_UPDATE=1` a load timeout rewrites `run/` and `out/` goldens with "timed out after 60 s" (seen: `out/results-fail.json`, `run/assert-fails.test.stdout`, `run/suq-money.test.stdout`, plus a new `run/suq-money.test.exitcode`); the update mode should not record a timeout as expected output.
- rv11 review: other messages still print `set.DisplayName` unqualified and can show look-alike sets (BYN2604 in `FlowAnalysis.CheckPropagation`, BYN2227 `AmbiguousVariant`, the `except` BYN2603 variant text, pattern-mismatch messages); switch them to `DisplayNameAmong` when one reproduces.
- rr6: `byn_release` (`runtime/bayan-rt.c:854`) frees only for `&byn_general`; a future allocator that does not free through it would bring back the `ListReplace` leak.
- 007 T1: a function-typed parameter without parentheses (`bool any(Token -> bool p)`) is accepted silently; decide whether it gets an input-form diagnostic with a fix to `(Token -> bool) p` (Q363 d).
- 007 T3: a function type as a `union` variant payload field gets BYN3002-style `BorrowPayload`, not BYN2822 (`CheckUnionShape` has no placement check).
- 007 T3: an explicit type argument on a generic function call (`f<i32 -> bool>()`) and an inferred one are not checked for BYN2822; only `TypeResolver.Construct` and the builtin generics are.
- 007 T3: `FunctionType` has no excluded cases, so `fails` naming a `union error … except .c` alias keeps the excluded cases in the function type.
- BYN2822 help "take the function as a parameter instead" reads wrong when the rejected position is already inside a parameter (`void a(Pred? p)`); found in 007 T3 review.
- 007 T5: `become` inside a lambda builds a `BoundThis` (`BinderStates.cs:35`) without the BYN2821 `this` check.
- 007 T5: the caught error (`_caught`, `CaughtErrorExpr`) read inside a lambda in a `catch` body is not recorded as a capture.
- 007 T5: `pick([], by: it > 0)` reports a cascading BYN2218 on `[]` after BYN2740 (existing recovery in `BindArgumentsForRecovery`).
- 007 T5: a user local named `it` in a function that also uses the `it` shorthand gives BYN2007 (shadowing) at the shorthand.
- 007 T4 review: a built-in or generic function passed to a generic function-typed parameter (`holds(print)` with `bool holds<T>((T -> bool) p)`) is BYN2740 "cannot infer" instead of BYN2824; `Instantiate` in BinderCalls.cs should run `ConvertToFunction`'s check on a non-qualifying group.
- 007 T4 review: the overload lookup in `ConvertToFunction` (BinderExpressions.cs, `fn.Module.Functions…fs`) is dead while a second module function of the same name is BYN2006.
- 007 T7: `-Wall -Werror` on emitted C still fails outside the lambda code: `-Wunused-label` (`continue_N` of a `for` with no `continue`, seen in a lambda body in `run/lambda-uses-provide`) and `-Wparentheses-equality` from `fail … if` (`if ((p_s.len == 0))`, `run/lambda-fails`); T9 territory.
- 007 T6: BYN3002/BYN3003 on a lambda region keep the generic help ("copy the data with `.copy(to: alloc)`") and label ("freed at the end of its block"), which fit a lambda kept past its loop iteration poorly; a lambda-specific help needs a branch in `CheckOutlives`/`CheckReturn`.
- 007 T8: BYN6005 messages name the synthetic lambda (`main$lambda10`, `stored$lambda1`); name it as "the lambda on line N" as region diagnostics do.
- 007 T11: BYN2821 says 'a `out` parameter' / 'a `in` parameter'; use 'an'.
- 007 T13: SPEC §21.5's BYN1028 row says "(help text)" though BYN1028 now offers a fix; GRAMMAR's input-form table has no `delegate` (BYN1028) row; the round-3 plan's "Total codes" row still counts 12 spec-only codes.
- 007 T13: SPEC §1057 (`Panic.handler`) says the lambda form "needs §3.16"; 007 stores only non-capturing lambdas, so say whether `Panic.handler = p => …` is accepted now.
- CEmitter: every `for` loop emits an unused `continue_N:` label, rejected by `-Wunused-label` under `-Wall -Werror` (008 T8, 2026-10-25).
- CEmitter: `let _ = expr` emits an unused C local, rejected by `-Wunused-variable` (008 T8, 2026-10-25).
- CEmitter: `if n == 0` emits `if ((p_n == INT64_C(0)))`, rejected by clang `-Wparentheses-equality` (008 T8, 2026-10-25).
- `bayan run <file>` inside `tests/cases/run/` builds the whole directory as one program; single-file run needs a copy elsewhere (008 T6, 2026-10-25).
- Under `as: .debug` a `string` (and so a `Symbol`) prints bare inside tuples, optionals and maps but quoted and escaped inside structs (pre-existing; found in the 008 review, 2026-10-25).
- A repeated `case "a"` in a `switch` on a `string` or `Symbol` gets no duplicate/unreachable diagnostic; the first arm silently wins (pre-existing; 008 review, 2026-10-25).
- `List<string>` and `List<Symbol>` cannot be formatted in an interpolation hole (BYN2225) (pre-existing; 008 review, 2026-10-25).
- A struct literal cannot name its type through a module: `lexer.TextSpan { … }` is BYN1001 (Q431) (009 T1, 2026-10-26).
- C# `ParseStatement` builds `WhileLetStmt` and `LabeledStmt` with `SpanFrom(start)` evaluated before `ParseBlock()`/`ParseStatement()`, so their spans end before the body; `stmts.byn` matches it (009 T7, 2026-10-26).
- C# `ParseFunctionType` builds `FailsClause`/`UsesClause` with `SpanFrom(start)` before `ParseClauseList` runs, so in a function type the clause spans only the keyword (Parser.cs 2006/2011; declarations at 1144/1152 span the list); `types.byn` matches it (009 T6, 2026-10-26).
- C# parser spans end early where `SpanFrom` is an argument before the parse it should cover: a block-bodied `LambdaExpr` ends at `=>`, a `MapEntryExpr` at `:`, a named format spec's `Argument` at `:`; matched in `exprs2.byn` (009 T10, 2026-10-26).
- ~~run cases `io-list-dir`, `io-invalid-utf8`, `io-copy-file` (tests/cases/run/) leave `a.txt b.txt C.txt bad.txt copy.bin empty.bin input.bin` in the repo root after a full suite; not gitignored (found in 009 T15 gate)~~ (superseded by the later root-leftovers entry; triage 2026-11-09)
- 010 hazard (009 review): the port's AST keeps source spans where C# rewrites node text (`int`→`i32`, rest params → `ReadOnlySpan`, missing return → `void`, lowercased attribute names, format-spec names `as`/`digits`); `dump2.byn` fakes the C# text at print time (`cSharpTypeName`, `dump2.byn:211`). Semantics must read names through the parser's rename helpers, not raw spans.
- 009 tests spawn about 4,500 `bayan-parse` processes per suite and T3/T12 per-class parser builds are not `Lazy`, so each class rebuilds (`src/tests/SelfhostParserT3Tests.cs`, `SelfhostParserT12Tests.cs`).
- `src/parser/main.byn` `parseOne`/`parseTimed` allocate without an arena, so `--many --time` over a large corpus grows memory per file (009 review).
- C# also takes `NotPattern`, `VarPattern` and block `PropertyDecl` spans before their last child (Parser.cs 4102, 4126, 1050); the port copies it (Q511). Fix in C# and the port together.
- `LexerTests.OneMegabyteAllocatesOnlyTheTokenArray` fails intermittently in full-suite runs (passes alone); 010 T2 gate saw 1–2/2629 failures, likely allocation noise from parallel tests (2026-10-26).
- 010 T3 gate: `typeEquals` (types.byn:195) is never called by tests; equality pairs lack same-name nominal types in different modules and text-view/builtin types (2026-10-26).
- spec 010 T28: compile.byn:1176-1177/:1180-1181 cite "generics.byn:180" for T13's drain switch (now ~generics.byn:186); ctx.byn:276 calls `quiet` "the C# `_quiet`", which does not exist (Q580). Fix at integration.

- K6 (011 item 4): on Windows, `FilePath` (`csharp/Bayan.Backend.C/CEmitter.cs:392-393`) passes an absolute path into `#line` and the file statics when the file is on another drive than the package root: `Path.GetRelativePath` then returns the absolute path, which does not start with `..`. The 011 port matches it (spec non-goals); fix in C# with a test, then the port.
- Q745: a borrow escapes through a `ref`/`out` argument the callee writes (`RegionAnalysis.cs:457-464`); arena string read after free prints NULs. Fix in both checkers before 011 T4 (Q745). Closed for `ref`/`out` and returned lends of a parameter's own storage by A #2 (StorePair `lends`; cases `diag/region-transfer-*`); the runtime and test lines below stay open.
- Q745 (d): a debug build (`byn_own`) does not catch an arena string escaping through an `out` argument: `tmp/q745-probe/a1-out` prints three NULs in `safe` and `debug` alike. Emitted C stays unchanged (A1); a run-time check for reads of a freed arena belongs to round 4 or 012.
- Q745: a growing `StrBuf` on the General allocator leaks every block it outgrows until exit (`runtime/bayan-rt.c:364` passes `old = NULL`; nothing frees the old block, on drop or chained). It is what lets `append` after `finish()`/`view` stay safe (`runtime/tests/strbuf-grow.c`), so freeing them needs the checker to refuse that append first.
- Q745: `SelfhostSemanticsT25Tests` and `SelfhostSemanticsT37Tests` reach `RegionAnalysis` internals by reflection (constructor, `Region`, `StoreInto`, `AllocatedFrom` by name and arity), so every signature change breaks them at run time; move them onto `InternalsVisibleTo` access so the compiler checks those calls.
- Region #2 follow-up (A, fix/region-soundness): a `ref` (not `out`) lending target reports BYN3005 on a single call (`collect(ref xs, to: ref v) { v.append(xs.span) }`), because the collecting pass leaves `v` owning `xs` before the reporting pass; conservative, pinned by `diag/region-transfer-ref-collect`. A fix needs the lend's position.
- Region #2 follow-up: broad callees (`t41IsBroad`: function values, interface methods, `extern`) record pairs with `lends: false`, so a lend of a `ref` argument's storage into another written argument is unseen there. Making them lend needs its own self-check of src/, std and examples.
- Region #2 follow-up: `let s = b.view` then `b.append(...)` on a `StrBuf` checks clean even without a call; decide whether that is a hole or by design (append after `view` stays safe while the General StrBuf leaks old blocks, todo Q745 StrBuf line).
- Region #2 review (MEDIUM, pre-existing on v0.4.0's driver too): an `out` argument whose location lives in a `ref` argument's storage (`var sp = xs.mutableSpan(); grow(out sp[0], from: ref xs)` where `grow` appends to `xs` then writes `v`) checks clean, so the callee writes freed storage; with the `out` second (`grow(ref xs, to: out sp[0])`) BYN3004 fires, so it depends on argument order. Needs the out argument's location root (through span indexing) in callBorrow's alias loop. Probes in .worktrees/fix-region-soundness/tmp/rv4/outalias-{swap,out}.
- Region #2 review (LOW): callBorrow's result-lend loop skips `pair.source == regionThis`, so a method returning a lend of a `this` field in a non-span type gets no lend at the call site; today the callee itself already reports BYN3003, so nothing escapes, but the recorded pair is unused.
- Region #3 review (LOW): a user callee that uses `alloc` and returns heap data is assumed to return arena memory even when its return summary says the result borrows only a parameter (`string pick(string a) uses alloc { …; return a }` in `provide` → BYN3002, clean before #3). Conservative; nothing in src, std or examples hits it. Fix: skip the context union when the callee's return summary names no allocation (needs a summary bit for "returns fresh allocation").
- Region #3 review (LOW): `effects.byn` `t42AnalyzeEffects` makes `c.allocates` in whatever allocator its caller provides, not necessarily `General` as the comment says; it is not in `T42Mark`. Safe while no caller wraps the semantic pass in a shorter arena (as for other Ctx lists); fix the comment or add it to `T42Mark`.
- Region #3 follow-up: interface methods and `extern` functions returning heap data under `provide` are assumed to allocate (no body for the effects pass); read the declared `uses` instead (`analyzeEffects` fills `allocates` only for functions with bodies).
- Effects hole (found by region #3 review 6): an allocating lambda or method group passed to a pure function-typed parameter (`string run(i64 -> string f)`, `run(n => $"v={n}")`) checks clean, while the same value bound by `let` is BYN6005. Region analysis now judges passed lambdas and functions by their own body (7e2d09bcc), but the effects checker should reject the argument: check passed lambdas and function refs against the parameter type's effect bound.
- Region #4(d) review (LOW): a struct passed by value next to a later argument that mutates it (`wtotal(w, at: w.push())`, `w` holding a `List`) checks clean; the copy shares the buffer pointer and C argument order is unspecified (clang evaluates `push` first, so it works today). `args[k].owners` of a plain struct local is empty; treat a by-value struct holding heap data as borrowing its local.
- Region #4(d) review (LOW): `check` on a `lib/std/<module>/` dir gives only BYN2014 (no entry point), so the plan's std self-check runs no region analysis there; check std through a test or a package entry instead.
- Region #3 review-9 (LOW): a lambda inside a generic struct member whose `templateOf` is null finds no template lambda (`t41LambdaTemplate` returns -1), so it is taken to allocate; a pure lambda there passed to an `alloc`-typed parameter under `provide` gives a false BYN3002. Map struct-member instances to their template too (`effects.byn` fill loop).
- Region #4(b) review-11 (LOW, pre-existing): a nested defer is missed. `defer { defer print(s[0]) }` then a later mutation is clean, but the inner body runs when the outer one does, at function exit. `lastUse` takes the innermost defer's run point (the end of the outer defer's body); chain outward to the outermost defer's run point (`region2.byn` `deferEnds`).
- Region #4(b) review-11 (LOW, pre-existing): mutations and loan creations inside deferred bodies keep their text position; only uses move. `defer xs.clear(); defer print(s[0])` (valid, LIFO) and `defer xs.clear(); print(s[0])` give a false BYN3004; `defer print(s[0]); defer for … xs.append(i); defer { s = xs.span }` is a missed use-after-free. Order deferred bodies by run point (LIFO per block) for mutations and `created` too.
- Region #4(b) review-11 (LOW): reassignment after a defer is not seen. `var s = xs.span; defer print(s[0]); s = ys.span; for … xs.append(i)` gives BYN3004, though the body reads `ys`. Same flow-insensitive class as #4(a); fold into #4(a)'s whole-store dominance.
- Region #4(b) review-11 (LOW): `lastUse` costs mutations × loans × uses × defers plus defers × blocks; 1,500 single-defer blocks in one function check in 1.08 s (0.29 s before), 3,000 in 7.31 s (1.04 s). The driver is unchanged (4.85 s vs 4.79 s). Precompute each use's run point once per function instead of per loan.

- MEDIUM (runtime audit, docs/security/2026-11-06-runtime-audit.md) Windows `CreateProcessW(..., bInheritHandles=TRUE, ...)` passes every inheritable handle to the child; use `STARTUPINFOEXW` with `PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_HANDLE_LIST` limited to stdin and the two pipe write ends.
- LOW (runtime audit, docs/security/2026-11-06-runtime-audit.md) `pipe()` then `fcntl(FD_CLOEXEC)` is not atomic and the pipe fds can be 1 or 2 when those are closed at startup; use `pipe2(O_CLOEXEC)` / `POSIX_SPAWN_CLOEXEC_DEFAULT` and keep the fds above 2.
- LOW (runtime audit, docs/security/2026-11-06-runtime-audit.md) panic, `todo` and `report_error` write untrusted text raw to stderr (terminal escape injection); route them through `byn_log`'s escaping.
- LOW (runtime audit, docs/security/2026-11-06-runtime-audit.md) `Io.readFile` and stdin `readAll` have no size cap (`/dev/zero`, FIFO, endless stdin exhaust memory); add a max-bytes limit or reject non-regular files.
- LOW (runtime audit, docs/security/2026-11-06-runtime-audit.md) `Path.combine(base, rooted)` drops `base` and `byn_path_full` resolves `..` lexically, so neither confines a path; document it and add a `Path.within(root, p)` check.
- LOW (runtime audit, docs/security/2026-11-06-runtime-audit.md) the `BYN_WRITE_TRUNCATE` writer cuts at `cap` without respecting UTF-8 boundaries, so a truncated panic message can be invalid UTF-8; back `room` off to a code-point boundary.
- Runtime: scripts should default to a never-freed `Arena` (SPEC :1278, round-4 batch 6 row 52); `bayan-rt.c` gives every program `General`.
- Spec: SPEC §13 (`async`/`await`, `Io.group`, `provide timeout:`) still predates the `scope`/`spawn` decision of 2026-11-04; rewrite by revision (0019 "Found, not applied"; R4D §7).
- Corpus scanners (SelfhostSemanticsT8Tests.OracleLeavesNoTempDirectory, SelfhostSemanticsNoPanicLawTests) fail on untracked `tests/cases/run/.bayan-staging-*` dirs left by killed test runs: skip dot-directories when scanning, or have the staging step clean up on start (found 2026-11-07, line-join suite).
- Docs (line-join gate note, 2026-11-07): BYN5103.md and the spec describe the `requires` exemption as struct-level only, but `Parser.cs:316`/`recover.byn:99` exempt every `requires` (an under-indented function-level `requires` is never flagged); align the docs with the code.
- Compilation.cs CNameOf: std.json and a user module std_json both map to C name std_json -> BYN4001 redefinition (existing a.b/a_b collision class; review 87637629..ed2c9616)
- LOW (016 review, 2026-11-07) `suq/storefront/checkout.byn:28` calls `Json.encode(patch)` on an `Untrusted<Address.Patch>` without `try`; `Json.encode` takes a `JsonValue` and fails `JsonError`, so the sketch needs a conversion and a `try` once it compiles.
- LOW (016 re-review, 2026-11-07) `lib/std/json/reader.byn:16-24` one `JsonError` serves parse and encode, so `Json.parse`/`JsonReader.next` callers that switch exhaustively need dead `nonFinite`/`nestedTooDeep` arms (`tests/cases/run/std-json.byn:14`); split into a parse and an encode error if a port finds this awkward.
- LOW (review cbe65e749683..19844a379ea9, 2026-11-07) BYN5103 compares indentation in bytes, so a tab counts as one column (`Parser.cs:323`, `recover.byn:171`): `    let n = s` then `\t\t.length` is flagged and the fix gives 8 spaces. Not fixed here.
- C# (review 017 T3 row 2, 2026-11-07): `Dist.Clean`/`Remove` (`Dist.cs:262`) lets `Directory.Delete(dir, recursive: true)` crash with an unhandled exception (exit 134) when an entry cannot be removed; report the failure and exit non-zero instead (selfhost `t3RemoveEntry` panics, Q1085).
- C# (review 017 T3 row 3, 2026-11-07): `Message.Text` formats with `CultureInfo.CurrentCulture` (`Messages.cs:9`), so `bayan cache stats` prints `1,5 MB` under a German locale; format with the invariant culture, as the self-hosted driver does (Q1081).

- `runtime/bayan-rt.c` `byn_io_run_with` (`bool group = timeout_ms >= 0`): a timed child sits in its own process group, so when it reads an inherited terminal stdin it stops on SIGTTIN and the call ends only at `.timeout`, and Ctrl-C no longer reaches it (an interrupted Bayan program leaves it running). - Site: inline code in `.page` bodies is not formatted by `bayan format` (only fenced examples are checked). 2026-11-16.
- wlt1 has no Python, so 3 `SelfhostSnapshotTests` fail there; install Python or skip them with a reason on hosts without it. 2026-11-16.
- `dist/macos-arm64/latest` is 0.2.0-dev (2026-11-05) and rejects the current clause order (BYN1065); rebuild with `scripts/dist.sh` after 0.3.2. 2026-11-16.

- Manifesto edition 1 is written against 0.2.0 (lines 9 and 1413) and its status badges reflect that; refresh the content for a later edition. 2026-11-16.
- `scripts/cleanup/run-all.sh:153` shows only `refuse` lines at the start of a line in its summary; indented `  refused:` lines from some scripts are missed. Gate note, 2026-11-16.

Waits for spec 019 to retire the C# oracle: C# outputs are frozen at the 0.3.2 tag (decision 006 §2).

- spec 026 writer: `runtime/bayan-rt.h:436` comment says user error variants start at 13; they start at 15 (built-ins IoError 1-11, Utf8Error 12, ParseError 13-14). Fix with spec 015 T15 part 2b, which bumps the runtime anyway (spec 026 T10 keeps `runtime/` unchanged).

- MEDIUM (owner decision; review 5810cf25b398..1d26738b, finding 1) `lib/std/fs/glob.byn` `walk` follows symbolic links to directories: `t/a/x.byn` + `t/c/up -> ..` returns `x.byn` 32 times, and two `-> .` links run 2^depth paths. No runtime or std query tells a link without following it (writer PLAN-CHANGE 2018c8ee), so the fix needs a new primitive (lstat-style `Io.isSymlink` or an entry kind from `listDir`), an A1 exception. Also: a symlink loop (ELOOP) is returned on a name match, since `fileExists` falls back to `lstat`. Decision 2026-11-18 (owner, Fable's recommendation): needs a new primitive; schedule after 019.
- LOW (review loop 2 of 5810cf25b398..1d26738b) `lib/std/fs/glob.byn:62-65`: an entry whose `stat` and `lstat` both fail is walked as a directory, so files inside a listable but unsearchable directory (mode `r--`) are dropped silently (`tmp/rv-g/src/d` at 644: 0 matches, at 755: 1). Decide with the symlink fix above (entry kind from `listDir`), or document it.
- LOW (same review) `lib/std/fs/glob.test.byn:57-62`: `locked.byn` is restored to 755 only if the test reaches the unlock; an aborted run leaves a mode-000 dir in `tmp/` that breaks the next run and `rm -rf tmp`. Restore with `defer`, or chmod before `touch`.
- LOW (same review) `lib/std/fs/glob.test.byn:59-60`: when the lock has no effect (root, or a chmod that does nothing under MSYS) the test sets `found` to the expected value and checks nothing; report it as a skip, and check `chmod`'s exit status.

- 019 full review LOWs (2026-11-23, range 16729ccae..feat/019, logs tmp/review019/; fixing task: T69c or 019b follow-up unless named):
  - src/driver/test.byn:288-295: `--jobs` accepts up to 2147483647, and `goldenJobs` (golden.byn:1421) caps it only at the case count.
  - src/driver/golden.byn:197-204: The sort looks each name up in `found` and takes the first match.
  - src/driver/golden.byn:1204-1215: The `--only` no-match check runs before the shard is validated.
  - src/driver/golden.byn:2355: `goldenEmit` never checks the `exit` it passes to `goldenBynCases`.
  - src/driver/golden.byn:651-654: The copy is placed outside `tmp/l009` only when `Path.relative` returns a `../` path.
  - src/driver/progress.byn:258-263: The test named "the timer cycles frames then clears" never checks that frames cycle. `frame >= 1 && shown` holds after a single tick.
  - src/driver/goldenprograms.byn:194 (with :163): If a package source cannot be read, it is staged as an empty file (`catch List<u8>()`), and the cache key also hashes `""` for it (:163).
  - src/driver/goldenexport.byn:539 (also :1448, :2611): `Io.run("chmod", …)` is called as `let _ =`: only a failure to start chmod is caught, and its exit code is ignored.
  - src/driver/goldenexport.byn:412, :420: When an input file cannot be read, `Io.readFile(...) catch List<u8>()` writes an empty file in its place and sets no `failure`.
  - src/driver/cinventory.byn:357: The `--emit-c` child runs through bare `Io.run`, with no timeout and without `goldenEnv`. That differs from every other child in these files, which goes through `goldenChild` with a deadline and `BAYAN_CACHE=tmp/cache`.
  - src/driver/mapmodel.byn:531: All 8 `@cases` of "index panics exactly for non-live keys" share one directory, tmp/map-model/l003-index, and each case deletes it first and last. The C# version used a per-run GUID work directory.
  - src/driver/mutate.byn:670: the work directory name is predictable (monotonic nanoseconds, where the existing route uses `t9Unique`), and the code runs `deleteWorkDir` then `createDirectory` with a gap between them in a shared `/tmp`.
  - src/driver/build.byn:165 (t5CoverageReport): `--counters <prefix>` merges every `<prefix>.*` file it finds and never removes them. Counter ids are not tied to the build that wrote them.
  - src/driver/build.byn:117 (t5Source with `--coverage`): with no `-o`, the instrumented program and its C go to the same `out/<mode>/<target>/` path as a plain build.
  - runtime/bayan-rt.c:4014: `drain_error = GetLastError()` runs after `WaitForSingleObject` returned WAIT_TIMEOUT, which does not set the last error.
  - runtime/bayan-rt.c:3157 (byn_io_remove, Windows): `byn_path_full` panics through `byn_path_fail` when `GetFullPathNameW` fails. Before this change the call returned an IoError.
  - src/driver/cache.byn:778 (zigCacheEnvFor): only `o/` is trimmed, and `<cache>/zig` sits outside `t9Evict`'s data directory, so `BAYAN_CACHE_MAX_MB` does not count it.
  - src/driver/mutate.byn:748-751 (`mutateWorker`): A worker that cannot start the command for one site returns `exitFailure` and drops all of its remaining sites.
  - src/driver/mutate.byn:219-224: An empty `BAYAN_MUTATE_JOBS` (set but "") is treated as invalid, not as unset.
  - src/driver/cache.byn:485-511: `<cache>/zig` is bounded only by the `o/` entry count. `h/`, `z/` and `tmp/` are never trimmed, and none of it counts toward `BAYAN_CACHE_MAX_MB`, since `evictWith` only looks at `v1`.
  - src/driver/bounds.byn:247-254 (`boundEvictCase`): `t9Evict(...) catch {}` swallows the error and the test compares only the names left.
  - editors/language-server/src/workspace/workspace.byn:914: The walk matches `.byn` names with `isBynName(name, as: caseless)`, and `caseless` is true on macOS. As far as I know, .NET `EnumerateFiles("*.byn")` matches case-sensitively on all Unix systems, macOS included.
  - editors/language-server/src/server/server.byn:649-655: `indexUri` indexes only URIs that `parseUri` returns as `.file`. The C# `IndexUri` (Server.cs) indexes `new Uri(uri).LocalPath` for any scheme that parses.
  - editors/language-server/sessions/record.sh:22-23,52-58: With `BAYAN_LSP` unset, record.sh still builds `Bayan.LanguageServer.csproj` with dotnet.
  - editors/language-server/sessions/record.sh:89 (reader thread): `int(v)` on a Content-Length the server sends that is not a number raises an exception, which kills the reader thread silently.
  - editors/language-server/sessions/l044.law.test.sh: Nothing in `.github/workflows` or `scripts` refers to `l044.law.test.sh` or `record.test.sh`. Only release.yml builds bayan-lsp.
  - editors/language-server/src/bounds/bounds.byn:12-19: `fresh()` deletes and recreates fixed directories `tmp/lsp-bounds/<name>` relative to the process's current directory.
  - scripts/ci/nightly-modes.sh:60,67: It does `rm -rf "$out"` / `rm -rf "$work"` on fixed paths under tmp/nightly-modes.
  - scripts/release/peak-rss.sh:13: It polls `while [ ! -s "$exitf" ]` and the background subshell writes exitf. There is no exit when the subshell dies without writing it, and no trap kills the measured PID.
  - scripts/pinned-bayan.sh:36: It hardcodes `${CC:-cc} ... -lm`, with no zig fallback and no Windows libs (bootstrap-build.sh adds -lshell32). It also reuses `.cache/bayan/<sha>` under the unchanged key.
  - scripts/test.sh (zig cache trim): It does `rm -rf` on entries in the shared `$root/tmp/zig-global/o` beyond the newest 2000 or older than 10 min, with no lock.
  - scripts/selfhost/land.sh:93-95: The regen step hardcodes `tmp/bootstrap/stage1/bayan` with no `.exe` suffix.
  - .github/workflows/laws-nightly.yml:112: `sed -n 's/^check: //p' docs/laws/028-*.md` takes every `check:` line in the file, not just the front matter (pre-push-laws.sh uses front()).
  - scripts/selfhost/pre-push-laws.sh (L043 check -> reexport-check.sh): Without BAYAN_LAWS_LEFT, the full tier runs reexport-check, which exits 1 when dotnet is missing.
  - .github/workflows/release.yml (bayan-lsp build step), scripts/dist.sh: bayan-lsp is built with no `--mode`, while bayan itself is built with `--mode safe`.
  - scripts/quality/coverage.sh: It writes into src/out/test and then does `rm -rf src/out/test`.
  - scripts/ci/golden-commit-check.sh:31: `grep -oE` consumes the boundary character on both sides of a match, so a hex token right after another matched token loses its leading boundary and is skipped
  - scripts/ci/nightly-modes.sh:181: `got` is set to "" when the exit code is 0, but `want` keeps the literal "0" read from the .exitcode file
  - scripts/pinned-bayan.sh:36: `"${CC:-cc}"` is quoted, so it is never split into words, and `-lm` is hardcoded with no Windows library. bootstrap-build.sh splits CC and adds -lshell32 on Windows
  - scripts/selfhost/reexport-check.sh:239: `sed 's\

- tools/llm-eval.byn `run` (~:1219-1246): with `--retry-diagnostics` the retry request's wallMs and tokens are not added to `speed.wallMs`/`tokens` nor recorded; retry runs under-report cost (review b76405c4ec96..83cce96471fa, LOW).
- scripts/evals/llm-eval.test.sh no-flag block: compares against the tool at hard-coded base 52b9b1ce; a later intended format change fails it, and a shallow clone without that commit fails instead of skipping (review b76405c4ec96..83cce96471fa, LOW).
- scripts/ci/pre-push.sh:29: detects ref support by grepping the golden script's usage comment; rewording it silently drops the published-ref exclusion (fails closed). A `--supports-refs` probe would not depend on comment text (review b76405c4ec96..83cce96471fa, LOW).
- runtime/bayan-rt.c:1442-1444, :2652: After a big allocation fills its own chunk, the next small allocation gets a new chunk twice that size. The decode case adds a shrink copy into a further chunk. (0.4.0 audit 01-runtime-memory #3, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- runtime/bayan-rt.c:446-447: Formatting an `f32` rounds twice (`(float)strtod`), so some printed values do not parse back to the same `f32`. (0.4.0 audit 01-runtime-memory #4, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- runtime/bayan-rt.c:3993, 4048-4058: After an abandoned join, the feed thread keeps the caller's `input` pointer. Pipe handles of drains that already finished leak. (0.4.0 audit 02-runtime-io #4, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- runtime/bayan-rt.c:2717, 2735, 2952, 2955, 3088, 4236-4242, 4495: Passing `ptr==NULL, len==0` to `memchr`/`memcpy`/`fwrite` is undefined behavior under C11. (0.4.0 audit 02-runtime-io #6, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- runtime/bayan-rt.c:2101-2102 (callers 3042, 3164, 3174, 3227, 3270): `byn_path_full` panics when `getcwd` fails, so the bool `Fs.fileExists` and the failing `Io.*` calls abort the process. (0.4.0 audit 02-runtime-io #7, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- runtime/bayan-rt.c:3785, 3798-3809: `PATH` entries are split on `;` but the `"` around an entry is kept. `C:tool.exe` is searched for in PATH instead of being treated as a drive-relative path. (0.4.0 audit 02-runtime-io #8, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/lexer/diagnostics.byn:304: `applyFix` builds `fromBounds(fix.end, until: getLineSpan(lastLine).end)` with no clamp, so it panics when `fix.end` falls inside a CR/LF terminator. (0.4.0 audit 03-lexer #1, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/lexer/source.byn:79-89: `getLinePosition` counts non-continuation bytes, so stray `0x80..0xBF` bytes are not counted as columns, while `decodeAt`/`runeCount`/`display` count each as U+FFFD. (0.4.0 audit 03-lexer #2, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/lexer/lexer.byn:381-389: Doc spans keep a trailing `\r` on CRLF files, and `docStart` survives intervening `//` comments. (0.4.0 audit 03-lexer #4, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/lexer/lexer.byn:999, :905, :976: `scanEscape`, `lexInterpolatedText` and `lexChar` stop only at `\n`, so CRLF leaves `\r` in tokens and diagnostics. (0.4.0 audit 03-lexer #5, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/parser/types.byn:98-105, 203-209: A `*` followed with no space by `>`, `,`, `)` or `?` is not taken as a pointer type, and `readonly u8*?` fails (0.4.0 audit 04-parser #4, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/parser/decls.byn:111, 257-263: Top-level statement attributes go through `parseAttributes` instead of `parseStatementAttributes`, and `GlobalStatement.span` leaves the attributes out (0.4.0 audit 04-parser #5, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/parser/decls.byn:1430-1431: `params ReadOnlySpan<T> name` reports BYN5008 with no fix instead of BYN1058 (0.4.0 audit 04-parser #6, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/semantics/calls.byn:1306: The bool-naming rule reads `.template` instead of `templateOf`, so `Box<bool>.set(true)` must be named (0.4.0 audit 07-semantics-types #6, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/semantics/express2.byn:1899: A constant `^` index of `i64.min` overflows inside the compiler (0.4.0 audit 07-semantics-types #10, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- manifest.byn:986-1094: `t10ReadValue` recurses with no depth cap, which breaks Rule 5 in memory-leaks.md:28-29. (0.4.0 audit 08-semantics-front #8, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- compile.byn:363, 583: `c.modulesByDir` keys on the import's spelling as written, so on a case-insensitive filesystem a case-variant import loads the same directory twice. (0.4.0 audit 08-semantics-front #9, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/emitter/cprint/builtin.byn:307-310: `bynh_replace_*` hardening: memmove(NULL,NULL,0), relational compare of unrelated pointers, no debug claim (0.4.0 audit 09-emitter #5, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/emitter/cprint/cctx.byn:61-73, src/emitter/cprint/expr.byn:813: `<math.h>` function-like macro names are not escaped in vtable calls (0.4.0 audit 09-emitter #9, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/driver/cc.byn:85-103: On Windows, `ccFindOnPath` can return a `.cmd`/`.bat` file. `byn_io_run_with` refuses batch files with permissionDenied (bayan-rt.c:4261-4266), and because `zig` is searched first, a `zig.cmd` anywhere on PATH blocks the `clang.exe`/`gcc.exe` fallback. (0.4.0 audit 10-driver-build #3, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/driver/cache.byn:914-950,706-712: `t9Evict` reads every cached file whole on every cache-miss build, although `Fs.fileSize` exists (bayan-rt.h:631, lib/std/fs). It also skips subdirectories, so `program.dSYM/` goes uncounted. (0.4.0 audit 10-driver-build #5, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/driver/cache.byn:1047-1068; bayan-rt.c:3160-3171: On Windows, `Io.remove` is `DeleteFileW`, which fails on a directory symlink or junction. `sweepStale` then calls `t9DeleteTree`, which empties the link target. (0.4.0 audit 10-driver-build #7, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/driver/audit.byn:51; walk.byn:118-121: `bayan audit <dir>` calls `bynFiles` with no bound, so it follows directory links out of `<dir>`, and the walk has no budget. (0.4.0 audit 10-driver-build #8, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/driver/commands.byn:34-36,74-75: `lex --json` is refused even though `lexer.dumpFile(..., json:)` supports it (src/lexer/main.byn:33-36). The refusal message points to the removed C# `bayan`. (0.4.0 audit 10-driver-build #9, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/driver/cache.byn:163-166: `clean --cache` removes only `cacheData()` (`<cache>/v1`) and leaves `<cache>/zig` (cache.byn:783). (0.4.0 audit 10-driver-build #10, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/sabk/macho/writer.byn:74-75: `exponent` overflows `v * 2` for `align > 2^62` and traps instead of failing `.badAlign`. (0.4.0 audit 12-sabk #2, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- src/sabk/enc/labels.byn:119-123: `emit` appends the `page21`/`pageoff12` reloc before `try word(...)`, so a refused `adrp`/`add` leaves a reloc with no instruction behind it. (0.4.0 audit 12-sabk #4, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- lib/std/path/glob.byn:21-46: `expand` also expands braces inside `[...]`, and an unclosed outer `{` stops expansion of a closed group inside it (0.4.0 audit 13-std #5, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- lib/std/fs/glob.byn:20: Depth counts every `/` in the pattern, including those in the base, so `walk` descends levels that can never match. Only time is wasted; results are filtered correctly (0.4.0 audit 13-std #7, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- lib/std/regex/regex.byn:472: `startsWith("[:")` rejects `[[:]`, which RE2 and Go accept (0.4.0 audit 13-std #9, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- editors/language-server/src/workspace/workspace.byn:305-330, :588: `unescaped("%00")` lets NUL into `localPath`. `key()` then calls `Path.fullPath`, which panics. (0.4.0 audit 14-lsp #3, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- editors/language-server/src/workspace/workspace.byn:490-496: `realPath` recurses once per missing component with no depth cap and copies the prefix at every frame, so memory grows quadratically (rule 5). (0.4.0 audit 14-lsp #4, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- editors/language-server/src/workspace/workspace.byn:876-878: `isLink(full)` runs on the already resolved `realPath`, so the check is always false. (0.4.0 audit 14-lsp #5, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- editors/language-server/src/server/server.byn:776; workspace.byn:878, :1287-1290: `dirExists` does a full `Io.listDir` (alloc plus qsort) before the exclusion test. A non-excluded folder is listed 3 times. A non-UTF-8 name makes `listDir` fail, so the dir is treated as missing (walk :919 also skips it). (0.4.0 audit 14-lsp #6, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- editors/language-server/src/transport/transport.byn:123-152: `read()` loops inside one message arena, so every skipped bad-JSON body (and each 64 KiB skip buffer for oversized messages) stays allocated until a good message arrives. (0.4.0 audit 14-lsp #7, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- editors/language-server/src/workspace/workspace.byn:798: `load()` runs `isLink` only when `!known`, so an indexed file swapped for a symlink is read through the link and re-indexed. (0.4.0 audit 14-lsp #8, LOW; docs/misc/2026-11-25-v0.4.0-audit.md)
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml:120-136,176-184`, `scripts/ci/known-failures.py:53-77`: A Windows shard whose report has no `test:` summary line still passes the known-failures check, because the reports are combined before it runs.
